## Status legend

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| ✅ PASS | Executed; latest verdict PASS (may carry minor copy/finding notes) |
| 🟡 PARTIAL | Executed with partial/limited evidence (source-confirmed, tooling-limited, or fixture-gapped sub-leg) |
| 🔴 STILL OPEN | Latest verdict FAIL or BLOCKED with no later PASS re-verification — real residual defect or env/fixture block |
| 📄 DOC-DRIFT | Guide assertion is obsolete/superseded; the underlying backend behavior was verified |
| ⏭️ SKIPPED | Attempted but explicitly not exercised (budget/persona/scope) |
| 🗑️ REMOVED | Feature/screen deleted from the product; documented disposition (status added QA Task 31d), not a defect or a run |
| 🔁 RETIRED | In-app flow removed/superseded — coverage moves to a referenced successor; documented disposition (31d), not a run |
| 🚫 NOT-SUPPORTED | Case asserts a capability the product deliberately does not provide (verified by design/DB); documented disposition (31d), not a defect |
| 🚫 N/A | Not applicable — the referenced integration/provider is not configured or has no UI option in this deployment (31d) |
| NEVER RUN | **Remaining** — no report on disk asserts a verdict under this canonical ID |

The real defect was the **inverse** — the live clothing rule taxed clothing **≤ $50** and exempted **> $50**. Corrected live 2026-09-12: ≤ $49.99 exempt · $50.00 and over taxable at the node rate · exactly **$50.00 taxable**.

> - **F1 (the priority finding)** — Tax Settings is now **diff-aware**: only keys whose value actually changed are written (verified live: changing the rate rewrote 1 row and left `sales_tax_enabled`'s `updated_at` untouched), an unrelated save can no longer coerce an unread key to `false`, and flipping the platform-wide switch **OFF** now requires an explicit confirmation step (Cancel restores it and writes nothing). Also fixed/test-id'd for the next round: Tax-Nodes inline validation instead of `alert()` (**F8**), review counters correct on all 13 rows (**F9**), `trades-search-input` test id (**F10**), seller profile no longer renders "(1 reviews)" (**F11**), completed-trade **Review CTA hoisted above Payment Details** (**F13**).

> **1. Guide corrections (item 5, each claim re-verified against the shipped build):**
> - **Q10/Q11/Q13/Q14 → NOT IMPLEMENTED.** Their old reason — "time-dependent / descoped" — was **wrong**. There is no edit-review path at all (no route, no Edit affordance, no `updateReview` in `src/services/review.ts`), no 30-day same-counterparty cooldown, and no 24-hour post-completion lock. The 24-hour **edit** rule *does* exist, but as an RLS policy in `supabase/migrations/030_reviews.sql`, so the DB guard is real while the app-side entry point is absent — meaning neither Q10 nor Q11 is drivable. **Q10/Q11 are backlogged as an app-only feature**; Q13/Q14 need a product decision (build vs retire).
> - **Q19 retitled "Admin keeps (unhides) a reported review"** and its copy/button corrected to the shipped **Keep** flow ("This will keep the review visible, reject all reports, and notify everyone who reported it."). Its precondition was wrong too: the queue lists any review **with report rows**, not only auto-hidden ones (auto-hide at 3+ reports is unreachable by construction — see Q16).
> - **Q20 retitled "Admin hides a reported review"** (was "…permanently deletes…"); no delete affordance ships. ⚠️ **Newly source-verified while rewriting it:** the Hide route deliberately leaves the `review_reports` rows in place, and the queue is built from `review_reports` — so a hidden review **stays listed in the moderation queue** (badged "Hidden") and the queue total does **not** drop. Only **Keep** removes a row from the queue.
> - **Copy-consistency sweep in the same pass:** **Q18's** precondition (it also demanded an auto-hidden review) and the guide's requirement-mapping table (Q19/Q20 titles, plus the stale "date presets" / "7 report types" rows for **P05/P06** that the FIX-Task-20 body corrections had left behind).
> **2. Code fixes (QA findings N1, N4, N5, N6 + observation N9):** the false "You can edit your review within 24 hours of submission." promise is **removed** from `SubmitReviewScreen` (**N1**); the review CTA's `accessibilityLabel` now matches its visible text and the SubmitReview title receives the counterparty's **actual name** on the in-app path (**N4**); the tax kill-switch banner speaks in the pending tense until it is saved (**N5** — and the TC-P04 Playwright spec, which passed only because it asserted that pending copy, now drives the confirm modal and asserts both tenses); `/reviews` refetches after every moderation action and shows a freshness/remaining label (**N6**); the public SellerProfile no longer prints "Completed Trades 0" beside a non-zero review count (**N9** — root cause: `trades` SELECT is participant-scoped while `reviews` is publicly readable, so a stranger's count is structurally 0; the card is suppressed at 0 pending a public-stats RPC).
> **3. Scope decision on item 1:** the edit-review capability was deliberately **NOT built** — Samer chose to remove the promise now and backlog the feature for a dedicated session. Because the DB guard already exists, the remaining work is app-side only (no migration).
> **4. O-1/O-2/O-3 sub-case rows — acknowledged, NOT yet materialized (item 6; flagged by QA in two consecutive rounds).** The gap is real: the guide's index carries **43** sub-cases (O-1 C01–C17 · O-2 C01–C12 · O-3 C01–C14) while this tracker holds only the three legacy aggregate rows (`TRD-TC-O1/O2/O3`), so a sub-case verdict currently lives **only** in the round bullets above and can never be row-flipped — which is precisely why the O-2 **C12 FAIL** had to be recorded as prose. **Plan for a dedicated pass (≈1 session):** (a) materialise 43 rows keyed `TRD-TC-O1-Cxx…`, seeded from the 2026-09-11/12 bullets + the Android-coverage map — the bullets already reconcile **exactly** (O-1 13 ✅ + 1 🟡 + 3 not-reached + 1 guide-deferred = 17; O-2 4 ✅ + 4 🟡 + 1 FAIL + 3 not-driven = 12; O-3 6 ✅ + 6 🟡 + 2 not-driven = 14), so this is transcription, not re-testing; (b) retire or re-point the three legacy aggregate rows so O-1/O-2/O-3 are counted once; (c) reconcile the documented TRD total (288) and the §1 roll-up arithmetic in the same pass. **Do not start this while an O-group round is mid-flight** — the new rows would immediately inherit the same prose-only verdicts.

Android reachability therefore required MANUAL SWIPES. **NEW FINDING 2 (LOW–MEDIUM, copy/state):** `ChatScreen` renders the frozen-chat state on FIRST mount before `trade` resolves — `isTradeActive = trade?.status === 'pending' || 'in_progress'` (L756) is false while `trade` is null, so a never-messaged in-progress thread briefly showed **"This chat is no longer active. The trade has ended."** (L839-844) + placeholder "Chat is no longer active" (L975) behind the safety modal; re-entry (trade cached) did not reproduce it. Recommend gating the frozen branch on `!loadingTrade`. **NEW FINDING 3 (LOW, dev-only):** `console.error('[chat.getTotalUnreadMessageCount] Error:', …)` (`chat.ts:560`) fires on a normal background path → dev LogBox banner over the Notification Center (FIX-2 class; not user-facing in release). **NEW FINDING 4 (LOW, UX):** with the safe-meetup card EXPANDED mid-scroll, its `safe-meetup-cta` can land under the floating tab bar on BOTH platforms (Android y2145–2272 vs tabs 2190+; iOS y852–898 vs tabs 868+) — reachable after further scrolling, i.e. no permanent occlusion, but worth folding into the measured-clearance fix. **NEW FINDING 5 (LOW, copy):** the Notification Center's error state renders a raw HTTP status (**"Gateway Timeout"**) under "Something went wrong" (§6.3 raw developer-string class; transient staging gateway error, Retry affordance present). **DOC-DRIFT (I02):** the guide expects the card EXPANDED by default and a *different* in-progress trade to show it expanded; the shipped design (FIX-Task-7 item 5b, `SafeMeetupCard.tsx` L66/70-80) starts it COLLAPSED for every trade and only ever persists `true` — so the dismissal "persistence" is indistinguishable from the default state and the guide's per-trade-expansion assertion is structurally unachievable. **Part 2 (iOS):** toggle expands → cta collapses → navigate away + return = card stays COLLAPSED (persistence confirmed as asked). **Part 3 (5c):** emoji chip VISUALLY CONFIRMED on real notification rows (iOS Notification Center) — 🧸 on the "LEGO Star Wars Set" Trade Cancelled row, 🎮 on "Nintendo Switch Games Bundle", 🚲 on "Kids Bicycle - 20 inch", a deterministic fallback glyph on the unbranded "QA Bundle Fixture 1 of 1" row, and NO chip on non-item rows (`new_message`, `offer_accepted`, `sp_spent`/`sp_refunded`) — matching the keyword map in `notificationItemGlyph.ts`.

**2026-09-10 — FIX-Task-13 close-out (P1 timeline occlusion + banner threshold + guide copy + 4 UX):** code + docs + **iOS on-device verification**; Android leg BLOCKED by an emulator harness fault (see below). **Item 1 (P1) — mechanism RECLASSIFIED, iOS reachability PASS:** with a fresh `qa:r41-in-progress-trade -- create --with-auto-complete` fixture and the NEW `qa-scroll-to?testID=safe-meetup-toggle` registry entry, the iOS AX tree shows `safe-meetup-toggle` y541–595, `message-button` y477–525, `report-problem-button` y607–655 and `request-cancel-button` y667–715 — **all clear of the pinned footer (y766–834) and the tab pill (y868+)**; Payment Details (`timeline-payment-tax-preview` y367) also renders above the footer. So the I02 band is **not permanently occluded** — the earlier Android FAIL is best explained by the timeline ScrollView's small scroll range (measured live: viewport 825 / content 930 / **maxScrollOffset ≈ 105pt**) plus QA having no scroll-to tooling for those two elements, which FIX-Task-13 now provides. **I02: 🔴 OPEN → 🟡 PARTIAL** (reachability re-confirmed on iOS; per-trade dismissal not re-driven; Android leg unverified). **I05: 🟡 PARTIAL → ✅ PASS** (positive leg now driven on iOS: `message-button` → Chat → safety modal → **composer enabled + `quick-replies-toggle` enabled + chips `quick-reply-chip-today/-tomorrow/-suggest/-more` render**). **Android leg = ENVIRONMENT BLOCKED, not an app defect:** the app ran the new bundle (log `[ENTRY] registerRootComponent` + `[NAV] route: TradeTimeline` + the new `[TradeTimeline][FIX-Task-13] scroll metrics` line) but the Trade Timeline's trade fetch never resolved in that emulator session for either fixture, while the same build + same trade rendered correctly on iOS — recorded as a driver/harness artifact per the on-device discipline rule. Also landed: AutoCompleteBanner now consumes the shared `AUTO_COMPLETE_URGENT_MINUTES` from `countdown.ts` (2-band design kept, divergence documented) + a non-colour "Due soon" cue; the "Need more time?" extension card auto-collapses behind a link until ≤6h; the step-1 "Message the seller/buyer" row is tappable; Notification Center rows carry a deterministic per-item emoji chip; guide copy for G01/G02/I04/D04/Y05 reconciled to shipped strings.

Its immediate JSX sibling `message-button` (L2014-2033) was likewise absent. The AX dump DOES list other below-fold content (e.g. `Need more time?` at y2390), so the omission is not simply "off-screen". Suspected root cause (needs dev confirmation): the FIX-Task-7 **pinned footer** (`confirm-trade-button`, y1921–2106 on a 2400px screen) overlays the ScrollView exactly where these two siblings render, so they are permanently occluded; content near the tail (Payment Details / Estimated Sales Tax) also renders beneath the floating tab bar.
